- **a2a**: `/a2a` is the slash interface to the agent2agent inter-session message bus. It claims (`open`) an inbox name and (on Tier-1 platforms) dispatches a single immortal background watcher — via `Bash(run_in_background: true)` — that exits only on a real message (or inbox-gone / lock-contention); it does not recycle. Newly arriving messages surface within ~3s WITHOUT any user-visible polling chatter. No ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/a2a.md) · ★13)
- **a2a**: agent2agent inter-session message bus. Triggers: '/a2a', 'open inbox', 'close inbox', 'listen for messages', 'listen as', 'send a message to session', 'check inbox', 'reply to that session', 'inter-agent chat', 'inter-agent messaging', 'agent2agent', 'a2a', 'agent bus', 'message another session', 'tell session Y to', 'ask session Y'. Subcommands: open, close, send, check, read, peek, names, bound-name. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/a2a.md) · ★13)

- **accounting-audit**: Audit specific accounts or transactions — vouching, tracing, recalculation, confirmation and analytical procedures — with each finding's cause, effect and fix. - **accounting-close**: Close revenue, expense and dividend accounts to retained earnings and prepare the post-closing trial balance that opens the next period. Not for starting a period's close from source documents; see /accounting-analyze. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/accounting-close.md) · ★4)

- **activity-monitor**: Wraps a target script/command and renders a live step checklist (✅/⏳/⬜/⚠️/❌) instead of raw stdout, so watching a long background run costs tokens proportional to real signal, not log volume. Known `AGENTIC_SUCCESS_BLOCK`/`AGENTIC_ERROR_BLOCK` markers are recognised mechanically, for free; anything else falls back to one cheap-model narration call per batch. Pass/fail is always the wrapped command ([OstMutant/Advertisement](https://github.com/OstMutant/Advertisement/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/activity-monitor.md))
